# Remaining Factory work

Current product owner: [LevitateOS/factory](https://github.com/LevitateOS/factory).
This is the single current task list for finishing Factory, consolidated on
2026-11-08 from the milestone queue, assumptions audit, security handoff and
freshness requirements after redesign D1–D8. It includes unfinished behavior,
qualification, release work and unresolved decisions. On 2026-11-08 the owner
explicitly started the goal to execute Q01–Q30 in order. Factory is not finished.
Source/fixture completion never substitutes for connected, installed or security
qualification.

**Current snapshot (2026-11-12): INCOMPLETE, GitHub/Muse verification active.** The
owner has authorized full GitHub verification; Fountain remains outside this
pass. The [active recovery ledger](../untangle-codebase.md#retained-review-recovery-fix-2026-11-11)
records current checks, installed state and original external-fixture receipts;
Q17 below retains earlier scoped evidence. No whole product or
security gate is closed by source checks. Use the
[consolidated GitHub/Muse todo](#githubmuse-consolidated-todo-2026-11-12)
below as the current scoped continuation; dated Q prompts remain requirements
and historical evidence, not a competing current status list.

**Daybreak boundary:** ordinary security engineering and ordinary real admission,
isolation, custody/recovery checks stay in this queue. No whole security gate is
automatically Daybreak-only. Only work/checks that actually look like hacking
require a user-started Daybreak session under AGENTS.md. Record each such exact
check, why it needs that session, affected gate and evidence still needed. Do not
run it here, invent an adversarial suite, waive it or infer Daybreak access.
A piece may be COMPLETE for its stated ordinary scope with separately named
Daybreak checks still pending; its broader gate remains open. If the missing
check prevents required ordinary admission or a downstream journey, report the
specific affected action as blocked; continue work that does not use that admission.

### Queueing and interruption recovery

Queued requests remain outstanding across blockers, later messages and turn
boundaries. Recover existing operations, receipts, claims, cursors and processes
before effects. A prior COMPLETE reply/build/component check alone is insufficient;
reuse qualifying exact evidence and never replay an uncertain send or inference.

On interruption leave one concise recovery note in the existing active task
record: completed work, exact blockers and affected actions, owned edits, running
processes/uncertain effects and the next executable action. Keep original state
and human history. When an answer, implementation or evidence removes a blocker,
resume its retained work. A missing appliance does not block desktop packaging,
store migration or unrelated GitHub/Fountain code.
